34	const SNOW_THRESHOLD = 1500;
35	const DESERT_THRESHOLD = 1500;
36	const MUSHROOM_THRESHOLD = 100;

48	  tileX: number; tileY: number;
49	  belowSurface: boolean;
50	  zoneSkyHeight: boolean;
51	  zoneOverworldHeight: boolean;
52	  zoneDirtLayerHeight: boolean;

76	  tileX: 0, tileY: 0, belowSurface: false,
77	  zoneSkyHeight: false, zoneOverworldHeight: false, zoneDirtLayerHeight: false,
78	  zoneRockLayerHeight: false, zoneUnderworldHeight: false,
79	  zoneCorrupt: false, zoneCrimson: false, zoneHallow: false, zoneJungle: false,
80	  zoneSnow: false, zoneDesert: false, snowTileCount: 0, desertSandTileCount: 0, graveyardTileCount: 0,
81	  zoneGlowshroom: false, zoneMeteor: false,
82	  zoneGraveyard: false, zoneDungeon: false, zoneTemple: false, zoneBeach: false,
83	  zoneUndergroundDesert: false, townNPCCount: 0,
84	};
85	
86	const countSet = (counts: Map<number, number>, set: Set<number>): number => {
87	  let n = 0;
88	  for (const t of set) n += counts.get(t) ?? 0;
89	  return n;
90	};
91	
92	/** 以玩家中心扫描并计算全部 Zone 标志（对应 ScanAndApplyToPlayer 主链） */
93	export function scanScene(
94	  world: World, centerX: number, centerY: number, townNPCCount: number,
95	): SceneFlags {
96	  const st = world.store;
97	  const w = st.w, h = st.h;
98	  const underworldLayer = world.lavaLine || (h - 200); // Main.UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200
99	  const worldSurface = world.groundLevel;
100	  const rockLevel = world.rockLevel;
101	  const tileX = Math.floor(centerX / 16);
102	  const tileY = Math.floor(centerY / 16);
103	
104	  // 169×124 采样窗口（SceneMetrics.ZoneScanSize）
105	  const sx0 = Math.max(0, tileX - 84), sx1 = Math.min(w - 1, tileX + 84);
106	  const sy0 = Math.max(0, tileY - 61), sy1 = Math.min(h - 1, tileY + 62);
107	  const counts = new Map<number, number>();
108	  let sunflower = 0;
109	  let oceanSand = 0;
110	  const oceanTopY = (worldSurface + rockLevel) / 2 + 40; // oceanLevel（WorldGen.cs:4393）
111	  const beachDist = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06)); // beachDistance（WorldGen.cs:4407 近似）
112	  for (let y = sy0; y <= sy1; y++) {
113	    for (let x = sx0; x <= sx1; x++) {
114	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
115	      if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
116	      const t = st.type[i];
117	      counts.set(t, (counts.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
118	      if (t === SUNFLOWER) sunflower++;
119	      // isDesertBiomeSand && oceanDepths（SceneMetrics L376-380）：海洋沙不计入沙漠沙
120	      if (SAND_TILES.has(t) && y <= oceanTopY && (x < beachDist || x > w - beachDist)) oceanSand++;
121	    }
122	  }
123	
124	  // 聚合（AggregateTileCounts L603-670：含神圣/邪恶/猩红互减、向日葵 -10）
125	  let holy = countSet(counts, HOLY_TILES);
126	  let evil = countSet(counts, EVIL_TILES) + sunflower * -10;
127	  let blood = countSet(counts, BLOOD_TILES) + sunflower * -10;
128	  const holyRaw = holy;
129	  holy -= evil; holy -= blood;
130	  evil -= holyRaw; blood -= holyRaw;
131	  holy = Math.max(0, holy); evil = Math.max(0, evil); blood = Math.max(0, blood);
132	
133	  const jungle = countSet(counts, JUNGLE_TILES);
134	  const snow = countSet(counts, SNOW_TILES);
135	  const mushroom = countSet(counts, MUSHROOM_TILES);
136	  const meteor = countSet(counts, METEOR_TILES);
137	  const dungeonTiles = countSet(counts, DUNGEON_TILES);
138	  // DesertSandTileCount（SceneMetrics L665：sand - oceanSand；oceanSand 在扫描循环内按 oceanDepths 逐格计）
139	  const desert = Math.max(0, countSet(counts, SAND_TILES) - oceanSand);
140	  const graveyard = Math.max(0, countSet(counts, GRAVEYARD_TILES) - (sunflower >> 1));
141	
142	  // Zone 判定（CalculateZones L673-697）
143	  const zoneUnderworldHeight = tileY > underworldLayer;
144	  const belowSurface = tileY > worldSurface;
145	  const centerWall = st.inBounds(tileX, tileY) ? st.wall[st.idx(tileX, tileY)] : 0;
146	  const zoneDungeon = dungeonTiles >= DUNGEON_THRESHOLD && belowSurface && DUNGEON_WALLS.has(centerWall);
147	  const zoneDesert = desert >= DESERT_THRESHOLD;
148	
149	  // oceanDepths（WorldGen.cs:4390-4400：y <= (worldSurface+rockLayer)/2+40 且 x 距边 < beachDistance）
150	  const beachDistance = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06));
151	  const oceanTop = (worldSurface + rockLevel) / 2 + 40;
152	  const zoneBeach = tileY <= oceanTop && (tileX < beachDistance || tileX > w - beachDistance);
153	
154	  return {
155	    tileX, tileY,
156	    belowSurface,
157	    zoneSkyHeight: tileY <= worldSurface * 0.35,
158	    zoneOverworldHeight: tileY <= worldSurface && tileY > worldSurface * 0.35,
159	    zoneDirtLayerHeight: tileY <= rockLevel && tileY > worldSurface,
160	    zoneRockLayerHeight: tileY <= underworldLayer && tileY > rockLevel,
161	    zoneUnderworldHeight,
162	    zoneCorrupt: evil >= CORRUPTION_THRESHOLD,
163	    zoneCrimson: blood >= CRIMSON_THRESHOLD,
164	    zoneHallow: holy >= HALLOW_THRESHOLD,
165	    zoneJungle: jungle >= JUNGLE_THRESHOLD && !zoneUnderworldHeight,
166	    zoneSnow: snow >= SNOW_THRESHOLD,
167	    zoneDesert,
168	    snowTileCount: snow,
169	    desertSandTileCount: desert, // 已扣海洋沙（oceanDepths 门见上）近似——扫描窗内沙漠区不含海岸时与原版一致
170	    graveyardTileCount: graveyard,
171	    zoneGlowshroom: mushroom >= MUSHROOM_THRESHOLD,
172	    zoneMeteor: meteor >= METEOR_THRESHOLD,
173	    zoneGraveyard: graveyard >= GRAVEYARD_THRESHOLD,
174	    zoneDungeon,
175	    zoneTemple: centerWall === 87,
176	    zoneBeach,
177	    zoneUndergroundDesert: zoneDesert && belowSurface && SANDSTONE_WALLS.has(centerWall),
178	    townNPCCount,
179	  };
180	}
181	

```

26	/** SetDefaults 245-249 scale *= 0.5(NPC.cs:17943-17960)——族内所有像素偏移按此缩放 */
27	const SCALE = 0.5;
28	const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number) => (v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v);
29	
30	/** Boss 伤害/防御基值缓存(自由头切换 249 属性时从基值派生,防重复写膨胀) */
31	const baseStats = new WeakMap<Enemy, { dmg: number; def: number }>();
32	const baseOf = (e: Enemy) => {
33	  let b = baseStats.get(e);
34	  if (!b) { b = { dmg: e.def.damage, def: e.def.defense ?? 0 }; baseStats.set(e, b); }
35	  return b;
36	};
37	
38	/** 每实例附加槽位(原版 alpha/noTileCollide/localAI 等无对应 Enemy 字段) */
39	interface BodyX { alpha: number; noTC: boolean; dying: boolean; deadT: number }
40	interface HeadX { alpha: number; free: boolean; l0: number; l1: number }
41	interface FistX { alpha: number; noTC: boolean; state: number }
42	const bodyX = new WeakMap<Enemy, BodyX>();
43	const headX = new WeakMap<Enemy, HeadX>();
44	const fistX = new WeakMap<Enemy, FistX>();
45	const bodyXOf = (e: Enemy): BodyX => {
46	  let x = bodyX.get(e);
47	  if (!x) { x = { alpha: 255, noTC: false, dying: false, deadT: 0 }; bodyX.set(e, x); }
48	  return x;
49	};
50	const headXOf = (e: Enemy): HeadX => {
51	  let x = headX.get(e);
52	  if (!x) { x = { alpha: 255, free: false, l0: 0, l1: 0 }; headX.set(e, x); }
53	  return x;
54	};
55	const fistXOf = (e: Enemy): FistX => {
56	  let x = fistX.get(e);
57	  if (!x) { x = { alpha: 255, noTC: true, state: 0 }; fistX.set(e, x); }
58	  return x;
59	};
60	
61	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
62	// 族内查找(原版静态 golemBoss 索引的等价物:NPC.cs:6052 声明、:19449 每帧回填)
63	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
64	
65	const findBody = (game: GameHooks): Enemy | null =>
66	  (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 245) ?? null;
67	const findHead = (game: GameHooks): Enemy | null =>
68	  (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && (n.vanillaId === 246 || n.vanillaId === 249)) ?? null;
69	
70	/** GetMyBalance + 脱离神庙/丛林加成(NPC.cs:19450-19457 / :19173-19180 / :31274-31282):
71	 *  经典单人 = 1;getGoodWorld +2/+3(expert,略);玩家不在神庙/丛林带 或 在地表以上 → ×2。
72	 *  简化项:本工程无 ZoneLihzhardTemple/ZoneJungle 判定,仅保留"玩家在地表以上"一支
73	 *  (地下神庙内 = 1,与原版常态一致)。 */
74	const balanceNum = (game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): number => {
75	  let num = 1;
76	  if (player && player.cy < game.world.groundLevel * TILE) num *= 2;
77	  return num;
78	};
79	
80	/** 出生渐显(SetDefaults alpha=255;AI 内 alpha -= 10/2t,NPC.cs:19477-19485 / :31310-31317 / :19187-19194)。
81	 *  返回剩余 alpha;期间 ai[1] 由调用方冻结(本体/拳 0、挂载头 30)——蓄力计时不起跑 */
82	const fadeAlpha = (e: Enemy, x: { alpha: number }): number => {
83	  if (x.alpha <= 0) return 0;
84	  x.alpha = Math.max(0, x.alpha - 10);
85	  e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(254, x.alpha);
86	  return x.alpha;
87	};
88	
89	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
90	// 碰撞(本族私有拷贝:bossAI.collideGround 是 private;140px 宽本体需多列采样防半悬空)
91	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
92	
93	/** 落地/顶墙/侧墙判定(原版共享 Collision 的 AABB 近似:底/顶三列、侧墙三点) */
94	function collideGround(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks): void {
95	  const st = game.world.store;
96	  const cols = [e.x + 2, e.cx, e.x + e.w - 2];
97	  if (e.vy >= 0) {
98	    const ty = Math.floor((e.y + e.h) / TILE);
99	    let top = Infinity;
100	    for (const cx of cols) {
101	      const tx = Math.floor(cx / TILE);
102	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) top = Math.min(top, ty * TILE);
103	    }
104	    if (top < Infinity) { e.y = top - e.h; e.vy = 0; e.onGround = true; }
105	    else e.onGround = false;
106	  } else {
107	    e.onGround = false;
108	    const ty = Math.floor(e.y / TILE);
109	    let bottom = -Infinity;
110	    for (const cx of cols) {
111	      const tx = Math.floor(cx / TILE);
112	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) bottom = Math.max(bottom, (ty + 1) * TILE);
113	    }
114	    if (bottom > -Infinity) { e.y = bottom; e.vy = 0; }
115	  }
116	  const rows = [e.y + 2, e.cy, e.y + e.h - 2];
117	  if (e.vx > 0) {
118	    const tx = Math.floor((e.x + e.w) / TILE);
119	    for (const ry of rows) {
120	      const ty = Math.floor(ry / TILE);
121	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { e.x = tx * TILE - e.w; e.vx = 0; break; }
122	    }
123	  } else if (e.vx < 0) {
124	    const tx = Math.floor(e.x / TILE);
125	    for (const ry of rows) {
126	      const ty = Math.floor(ry / TILE);
127	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { e.x = (tx + 1) * TILE; e.vx = 0; break; }
128	    }
129	  }
130	}
131	
132	/** AABB 与实心块相交(原版 Collision.SolidTiles 近似):拳冲拳段关闭穿墙后的撞停判定 */
133	function hitSolid(game: GameHooks, e: Enemy): boolean {
134	  const st = game.world.store;
135	  const x0 = Math.floor(e.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((e.x + e.w - 0.01) / TILE);
136	  const y0 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((e.y + e.h - 0.01) / TILE);
137	  for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
138	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
139	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return true;
140	    }
141	  }
142	  return false;
143	}
144	
145	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
146	// 弹幕(原版 NewProjectile 调用点:id 258 火焰弹 / 259 眼激光)
147	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
148	
149	/** 嘴部/额前火焰弹(:31329-31347 / :31430-31447 / :31596-31617):
150	 *  从 (e.center + (ox,oy)) 朝玩家中心 proj 258 */
151	function golemSpit(
152	  game: GameHooks, e: Enemy, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number,
153	  spd: number, dmg: number, projId: number,
154	): void {
155	  const sx = e.cx + ox, sy = e.cy + oy;
156	  const dx = player.cx - sx, dy = player.cy - sy;
157	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
158	  shoot(game, sx, sy, (dx / d) * spd, (dy / d) * spd, dmg, projId);
159	}
160	
161	/** 挂载头眼激光(:31459-31484):双侧眼(±18*scale,-22*scale)各一发 11 速;
162	 *  玩家在头正上/下方(localAI[1]==0)→ 双眼齐射,否则朝向侧单眼(±30*scale)12 速;伤 28,proj 259 */
163	function golemEyeLasers(game: GameHooks, e: Enemy, player: Player, side: number): void {
164	  const fire = (ox: number, spd: number) => {
165	    const sx = e.cx + ox, sy = e.cy - 22 * SCALE;
166	    const dx = player.cx - sx, dy = player.cy - sy;
167	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
168	    const vx = (dx / d) * spd, vy = (dy / d) * spd;
169	    shoot(game, sx + vx * 3, sy + vy * 3, vx, vy, 28, 259, 300);
170	  };
171	  if (side === 0) { fire(-18 * SCALE, 11); fire(18 * SCALE, 11); }
172	  else fire(side * 30 * SCALE, 12);
173	}
174	
175	/** 自由头眼激光(:31659-31717):双眼(±14*scale,-50*scale)各一发 proj 259;
176	 *  伤 24/速 11,本体(此处用头自身血量 r)每降一档 +1 伤/+0.25 速;
177	 *  无视线(flag49)→ 伤 ×1.5、速 ×2.5、按玩家速度随机提前量瞄准 */
178	function golemEyeLasersFree(
179	  game: GameHooks, e: Enemy, player: Player, noLos: boolean, r: number,
180	): void {
181	  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
182	    const sx = e.cx + (i === 0 ? -14 : 14) * SCALE;
183	    const sy = e.cy - 50 * SCALE;
184	    let dmg = 24, spd = 11;
185	    if (r < 0.5) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
186	    if (r < 0.4) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
187	    if (r < 0.3) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
188	    if (r < 0.2) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
189	    if (r < 0.1) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
190	    let tx = player.cx, ty = player.cy;
191	    if (noLos) {
192	      dmg = Math.floor(dmg * 1.5);
193	      spd *= 2.5;
194	      const pvx = (player as unknown as { vx: number }).vx ?? 0;
195	      const pvy = (player as unknown as { vy: number }).vy ?? 0;
196	      tx += pvx * Math.random() * 50;
197	      ty += pvy * Math.random() * 50;
198	    }
199	    const dx = tx - sx, dy = ty - sy;
200	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
201	    const vx = (dx / d) * spd, vy = (dy / d) * spd;
202	    shoot(game, sx + vx * 3, sy + vy * 3, vx, vy, dmg, 259, 300);
203	  }
204	}
205	
206	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
207	// AI_045 石巨人本体 245(NPC.cs:19447-19722)
208	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
209	
210	/** 挂载头 → 自由态(原版 HitEffect :85842-85847:246 血尽原地 NewNPC 249;
211	 *  本工程头原体变身,满血+属性对齐 249 SetDefaults :12139-12149) */
212	function golemHeadGoFree(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks): void {
213	  const x = headXOf(e);
214	  if (x.free) return;
215	  x.free = true;
216	  e.ai3 = 1;          // 自由态标记(探针/部件锚定可观测)
217	  e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
218	  e.hp = e.maxHp;     // 原版 249 满血登场
219	  game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#C04030', 16, 2.6, { life: 30 });
220	  game.playSfx('roar', 0.8);
221	}
222	
223	export function golemAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
224	  const x = bodyXOf(e);
225	
226	  // ---- 血尽转换(首行):死亡演出 60t 后真死;头由自身 AI 转自由态续战 ----
227	  if (e.hp <= 0 && !x.dying) {
228	    x.dying = true; x.deadT = 0;
229	    e.ai3 = 1;          // 死亡标记(头据此转自由态)
230	    e.hp = e.maxHp;     // 满血续命防每 tick 重入
231	    game.playSfx('roar', 0.9);
232	  }
233	
234	  // ---- 首帧:localAI[0]==0 生成部件(NPC.cs:19458-19467) ----
235	  //  原版 NewNPC 传左上角坐标:拳 247=(Center-84,-9)、248=(+78,-9)、头 246=(-3,-57)
236	  if (!e.bInit) {
237	    e.bInit = true;
238	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
239	    e.alphaFade = false;           // 接管渐显(原版 AI 内 alpha -= 10)
240	    const spawn = (id: number, ox: number, oy: number, side: number) => {
241	      const p = spawnPart(game, e, id);
242	      if (!p) return;
243	      p.x = e.cx + ox; p.y = e.cy + oy;
244	      p.ai0 = side;                // 拳 ai0=侧别(-1 左/1 右;头不使用)
245	    };
246	    spawn(247, -84, -9, -1);
247	    spawn(248, 78, -9, 1);
248	    spawn(246, -3, -57, 0);
249	  }
250	
251	  // ---- 出生渐显(NPC.cs:19477-19485):期间 ai[1]=0,蓄力冻结 ----
252	  if (fadeAlpha(e, x) > 0) e.ai1 = 0;
253	
254	  // ---- 死亡演出:无敌+碎裂尘,60t 后真死(头/拳继续) ----
255	  if (x.dying) {
256	    e.iframes = 2;
257	    e.vx *= 0.9;
258	    e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAV, MAX_FALL);
259	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
260	    collideGround(e, game);
261	    x.deadT++;
262	    if (x.deadT % 6 === 0) {
263	      game.spawnParticles(
264	        e.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * e.w, e.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * e.h,
265	        '#B04030', 6, 2.4, { life: 36 });
266	    }
267	    if (x.deadT >= 60) { e.dead = true; return; }
268	    return;
269	  }
270	
271	  const num = balanceNum(game, player);
272	
273	  // ---- 部件存活扫描(NPC.cs:19487-19512):flag=头/flag2=左拳/flag3=右拳 ----
274	  //  注意头原体变身自由态后(ai3=1)视为"挂载头已失"——本体解除无敌且蓄力提速,
275	  //  对齐原版"246 死亡 → NewNPC 249"后 flag 为假的语义
276	  let hasHead = false, hasL = false, hasR = false;
277	  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
278	    if (n.dead) continue;
279	    if (n.vanillaId === 246 && n.ai3 !== 1) hasHead = true;
280	    else if (n.vanillaId === 247) hasL = true;
281	    else if (n.vanillaId === 248) hasR = true;
282	  }
283	  // dontTakeDamage = 挂载头在场(NPC.cs:19513):一阶段本体无敌,先打头/拳
284	  if (hasHead) e.iframes = 2;
285	  // getGoodWorld 落体砸火把(:19514-19534,expert 略)
286	
287	  // ---- 断臂尘(NPC.cs:19536-19595):原版每 tick 双簇 Dust+Gore,此处每 10t 一簇粒子近似 ----
288	  if (!hasL && e.aiT % 10 === 0) game.spawnParticles(e.cx - 80 * SCALE, e.cy - 9, '#9A8FA0', 3, 1.2, { life: 40 });
289	  if (!hasR && e.aiT % 10 === 0) game.spawnParticles(e.cx + 62 * SCALE, e.cy - 9, '#9A8FA0', 3, 1.2, { life: 40 });
290	
291	  // ---- 跳跃穿墙解除(NPC.cs:19569-19578):下落低于玩家顶 / 有视线且自身不在块内 ----
292	  if (x.noTC && player && !player.dead) {
293	    if (e.vy > 0 && e.y + e.h > player.y) x.noTC = false;
294	    else if (losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy) && !hitSolid(game, e)) x.noTC = false;
295	  }
296	
297	  // TargetClosest 的 direction 近似:朝玩家符号,同侧回退 facing
298	  const dir = player ? (Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || e.facing || 1) : 1;
299	  if (player) e.facing = dir;
300	
301	  if (player && e.ai0 === 0) {
302	    // ---- 地面蓄力(NPC.cs:19580-19645):ai[1] 攒 300 → -20 蹲伏 20t → ==-1 起跳 ----
303	    //  蹲伏段(负值)num4 固定 1;蓄力段按断臂/残血提速,再乘脱庙狂暴系数
304	    if (e.vy === 0) {
305	      e.vx *= 0.8;
306	      let num4 = 1;
307	      if (e.ai1 > 0) {
308	        if (!hasL) num4 += 2;
309	        if (!hasR) num4 += 2;
310	        if (!hasHead) num4 += 2;
311	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp) num4 += 1;
312	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num4 += 4;
313	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 3) num4 += 8;
314	        num4 *= num;
315	        // getGoodWorld:num4 += 100(:19613-19616,expert 略)
316	      }
317	      e.ai1 += num4;
318	      if (e.ai1 >= 300) e.ai1 = -20;
319	      else if (e.ai1 === -1) {
320	        // 起跳(:19624-19643):穿墙跳,vx=4*dir;残血跳更高 -12.1*(num+9)/10 钳 -19.1
321	        x.noTC = true;
322	        e.vx = 4 * dir;
323	        e.vy = e.hp < e.maxHp ? Math.max(-19.1, (-12.1 * (num + 9)) / 10) : -12.1;
324	        e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;
325	      }
326	    }
327	  } else if (player && e.ai0 === 1) {
328	    // ---- 跳跃段(NPC.cs:19646-19708) ----
329	    if (e.vy === 0) {
330	      // 落地(:19648-19661):Item14 音效 + 落地尘/Gore
331	      game.playSfx('roar', 0.35);
332	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#9A8FA0', 12, 2.2, { life: 40 });
333	      e.ai0 = 0;
334	    } else {
335	      // 玩家在身下水平跨度内 → 收横向速度(夹住玩家下压),否则朝玩家加速钳 ±num7
336	      if (e.x < player.x + player.w && e.x + e.w > player.x) {
337	        e.vx *= 0.9;
338	        if (e.y + e.h < player.y) e.vy += (0.2 * (num + 1)) / 2;
339	      } else {
340	        e.vx += 0.2 * dir;
341	        let num7 = 3;
342	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp) num7 += 1;
343	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num7 += 1;
344	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num7 += 1;
345	        num7 *= (num + 1) / 2;
346	        e.vx = clamp(e.vx, -num7, num7);
347	      }
348	    }
349	  }
350	
351	  // ---- 玩家死亡 vs 远离（NPC.cs:19469-19475 / 19709-19721）----
352	  // 玩家死：仅 noTileCollide=true（无 despawn 无状态切换，AI 继续跑）；
353	  // 活人曼哈顿 >3000：瞬消（二次判定 TargetClosest 后）
354	  if (!player) {
355	    x.noTC = true;
356	  } else if (Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) + Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 3000) {
357	    e.dead = true; e.bossFled = true;
358	    return;
359	  }
360	
361	  // ---- 重力+位移+落地(原版共享物理:noGravity=false → 0.3/2t、maxFall 10) ----
362	  e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAV, MAX_FALL);
363	  e.x += e.vx;
364	  e.y += e.vy;
365	  if (!x.noTC) collideGround(e, game);
366	  else e.onGround = false;
367	}
368	
369	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
370	// aiStyle 46 挂载头 246(NPC.cs:31273-31499)+ aiStyle 48 自由头 249(NPC.cs:31500-31741)
371	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
372	
373	export function golemHeadAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
374	  const x = headXOf(e);
375	  if (e.vanillaId === 249) x.free = true;   // 直接生成的自由头(原版 NewNPC 249)
376	  if (!e.bInit) {
377	    e.bInit = true;
378	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
379	    e.ai3 = x.free ? 1 : 0;
380	    e.alphaFade = false;
381	    x.alpha = x.free ? 0 : 255;             // 249 SetDefaults 无 alpha(:12139-12149)
382	  }
383	
384	  // ---- 血尽转换(首行):自由头死=战斗结束全族自毁;挂载头死=转自由态 ----
385	  if (e.hp <= 0) {
386	    if (x.free) {
387	      e.dead = true;
388	      for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
389	        if (!n.dead && n !== e && n.vanillaId !== null && n.vanillaId >= 245 && n.vanillaId <= 249) n.dead = true;
390	      }
391	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#C04030', 20, 3, { life: 40 });
392	      game.playSfx('roar', 1);
393	      return;
394	    }
395	    golemHeadGoFree(e, game);
396	  }
397	
398	  // ---- 属性:自由头按 249 SetDefaults(NPC.cs:12139-12149:damage 80/defense 32) ----
399	  if (x.free) {
400	    const free = vanillaNpc(249);
401	    e.def.damage = free?.damage ?? 80;
402	    e.def.defense = free?.defense ?? 32;
403	  } else {
404	    const b = baseOf(e);
405	    e.def.damage = b.dmg;
406	    e.def.defense = b.def;
407	  }
408	
409	  // 本体死亡/缺失 → 挂载头转自由态(本体死透后头继续)
410	  const body = findBody(game);

477	      if (r < 0.6) e.ai1 += num761;
478	      if (r < 0.2) e.ai1 += num761;
479	      if (r < 0.1) e.ai1 += num761;

508	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
509	// AI_047 石巨人拳 247/248(NPC.cs:19171-19446)
510	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
511	// 原版 ai[0] 是状态机(0 归位/1 蓄力/2 冲拳/3 直飞);任务约定 ai0=侧别,
512	// 状态机落在本文件 WeakMap(fistX.state),计时仍用 ai[1]。
513	
514	export function golemFistAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
515	  const x = fistXOf(e);
516	  if (!e.bInit) {
517	    e.bInit = true;
518	    e.ai0 = e.vanillaId === 247 ? -1 : 1;   // ai0=侧别(-1 左拳/1 右拳;由本体生成时预置)
519	    e.ai1 = 0;
520	    e.alphaFade = false;
521	    x.alpha = 255;
522	  }
523	  const isLeft = e.vanillaId === 247;
524	
525	  // ---- 锚主:golemBoss 缺失 → 自毁(NPC.cs:19182-19186) ----
526	  //  本体存活(含死亡演出)→ 锚本体;本体死透 → 锚自由头(任务约定"锚定本体(或头)")
527	  const body = findBody(game);
528	  const head = findHead(game);
529	  const anchor = body && !body.dead ? body : head;
530	  if (!anchor) { e.dead = true; return; }
531	  e.master = anchor; // 拳链渲染用（Renderer.drawGolemFistChain，Main.cs:22593-22632）
532	
533	  const num = balanceNum(game, player);
534	  // 渐显(:19187-19194):期间 ai[1]=0
535	  if (fadeAlpha(e, x) > 0) e.ai1 = 0;
536	
537	  // ---- 肩锚点(:19197-19199):锚主.Center + 锚主.velocity + (±84/78*scale, -9*scale) ----
538	  const offX = isLeft ? -84 : 78;
539	  const tx = anchor.cx + anchor.vx + offX * SCALE;
540	  const ty = anchor.cy + anchor.vy - 9 * SCALE;
541	  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
542	  const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
543	  e.facing = isLeft ? 1 : -1;               // 镜像朝向(原版贴图方向)
544	
545	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
546	
547	  if (x.state === 0) {
548	    // ---- 归位/待机蓄力(:19202-19270) ----
549	    x.noTC = true;
550	    let num3 = 14;
551	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num3 += 3;
552	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num3 += 3;
553	    if (anchor.hp < anchor.maxHp) num3 += 8;
554	    num3 *= (num + 3) / 4;
555	    if (num3 > 32) num3 = 32;
556	    if (dist < 12 + num3) {
557	      // 贴肩:整帧速度 = 偏移量(原版 velocity = delta),蓄力计时(:19216-19259)
558	      e.spin = 0;
559	      e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy;
560	      e.ai1 += num;
561	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) e.ai1 += num;
562	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) e.ai1 += num;
563	      if (anchor.hp < anchor.maxHp) e.ai1 += 10 * num;
564	      if (e.ai1 >= 60) {
565	        // 出拳侧别门(:19244-19256):左拳只在玩家偏左时出、右拳对称
566	        const sideOk = isLeft ? e.cx + 100 > player.cx : e.cx - 100 < player.cx;
567	        e.ai1 = 0;
568	        if (sideOk) x.state = 1;
569	      }
570	    } else {
571	      e.vx = (dx / dist) * num3;
572	      e.vy = (dy / dist) * num3;
573	      // 归位朝向(:19260-19262):默认 atan2(-vy,-vx),247 镜像为 atan2(vy,vx)
574	      e.spin = isLeft ? Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) : Math.atan2(-e.vy, -e.vx);
575	    }
576	  } else if (x.state === 1) {
577	    // ---- 蓄力:贴肩 30t(:19271-19332) ----
578	    e.ai1 += 1;
579	    e.x = tx - e.w / 2;                     // 原版 base.Center = vector(整帧钉在肩锚)
580	    e.y = ty - e.h / 2;
581	    e.spin = 0; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
582	    if (e.ai1 <= 15) {                      // :19278-19292 聚能尘(粒子近似)
583	      game.spawnParticles(
584	        e.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30, e.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30,
585	        '#C8A040', 1, 0.8, { life: 26 });
586	    }
587	    if (e.ai1 >= 30) {
588	      x.noTC = true;
589	      x.state = 2; e.ai1 = 0;
590	      // 冲拳速度(:19302-19321):12 + 残血/本体受损加成,×(num+3)/4 钳 48
591	      let num6 = 12;
592	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num6 += 4;
593	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num6 += 4;
594	      if (anchor.hp < anchor.maxHp) num6 += 10;
595	      num6 *= (num + 3) / 4;
596	      if (num6 > 48) num6 = 48;
597	      const pdx = player.cx - e.cx, pdy = player.cy - e.cy;
598	      const pd = Math.hypot(pdx, pdy) || 1;
599	      e.vx = (pdx / pd) * num6; e.vy = (pdy / pd) * num6;
600	      // 冲拳朝向(:19325-19328):默认 atan2(vy,vx),247 镜像为 atan2(-vy,-vx)(与归位相反)
601	      e.spin = isLeft ? Math.atan2(-e.vy, -e.vx) : Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
602	    }
603	  } else if (x.state === 2) {
604	    // ---- 冲拳飞行(:19333-19393) ----
605	    e.ai1 += 1;
606	    if (e.ai1 === 1) game.playSfx('roar', 0.4);   // SoundID.Item14(:19344-19346)
607	    if (e.aiT % 2 === 0) {                        // :19348-19356 焰尾尘(粒子近似)
608	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx + e.vx * 0.5, e.cy + e.vy * 0.5, '#E08040', 1, 0.6, { life: 20 });
609	    }
610	    // 过轴判定(:19363-19388):主轴冲过玩家 → 关穿墙(开始撞墙)
611	    if (Math.abs(e.vx) > Math.abs(e.vy)) {
612	      if (e.vx > 0 && e.cx > player.cx) x.noTC = false;
613	      if (e.vx < 0 && e.cx < player.cx) x.noTC = false;
614	    } else {
615	      if (e.vy > 0 && e.cy > player.cy) x.noTC = false;
616	      if (e.vy < 0 && e.cy < player.cy) x.noTC = false;
617	    }
618	    // 离锚 700 → 回位(:19389-19392;撞墙分支在位移后判定)
619	    if (dist > 700) { x.noTC = true; x.state = 0; }
620	  } else if (x.state === 3) {
621	    // ---- 直飞追击(:19394-19446;原版无处置 ai[0]=3 的代码,保留 1:1) ----
622	    x.noTC = true;
623	    const pdx = player.cx - e.cx, pdy = player.cy - e.cy;
624	    const pd = Math.hypot(pdx, pdy) || 1;
625	    e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (pdx / pd) * 12, 0.4);
626	    e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (pdy / pd) * 12, 0.4);
627	    e.spin = isLeft ? Math.atan2(-e.vy, -e.vx) : Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
628	  }
629	
630	  // ---- 位移(蓄力态已整帧钉锚;冲拳非穿墙段撞墙即回位) ----
631	  if (x.state !== 1) {
632	    e.x += e.vx;
633	    e.y += e.vy;
634	    if (x.state === 2 && !x.noTC && hitSolid(game, e)) {
635	      x.noTC = true;
636	      x.state = 0;                          // :19389-19392 collideX/collideY → 回位
637	    }
638	  }
639	}
640

		ZoneDirtLayerHeight = (double)TileCenter.Y <= Main.rockLayer && (double)TileCenter.Y > Main.worldSurface;
		ZoneRockLayerHeight = TileCenter.Y <= Main.UnderworldLayer && (double)TileCenter.Y > Main.rockLayer;
		ZoneUnderworldHeight = TileCenter.Y > Main.UnderworldLayer;
		ZoneCorrupt = EnoughTilesForCorruption;
		ZoneCrimson = EnoughTilesForCrimson;
		ZoneHallow = EnoughTilesForHallow;
		ZoneJungle = EnoughTilesForJungle && !ZoneUnderworldHeight;
		ZoneSnow = EnoughTilesForSnow;
		ZoneDesert = EnoughTilesForDesert;
		ZoneGlowshroom = EnoughTilesForGlowingMushroom;
		ZoneMeteor = EnoughTilesForMeteor;
		ZoneGraveyard = EnoughTilesForGraveyard;
		ZoneDungeon = EnoughTilesForDungeon && BelowSurface && Main.wallDungeon[tileSafely.wall];
		ZoneLihzhardTemple = tileSafely.wall == 87;
		ZoneGranite = tileSafely.wall == 184 || tileSafely.wall == 180;
		ZoneMarble = tileSafely.wall == 183 || tileSafely.wall == 178;
		ZoneHive = tileSafely.wall == 108 || tileSafely.wall == 86;
		ZoneGemCave = tileSafely.wall >= 48 && tileSafely.wall <= 53;
		ZoneBeach = WorldGen.oceanDepths(TileCenter.X, TileCenter.Y);
		ZoneUndergroundDesert = ZoneDesert && BelowSurface && (WallID.Sets.Conversion.Sandstone[tileSafely.wall] || WallID.Sets.Conversion.HardenedSand[tileSafely.wall] || tileSafely.wall == 223) && !Main.wallHouse[tileSafely.wall];
		SurfaceAtmospherics = WorldGen.IsSurfaceForAtmospherics(TileCenter);
		if (Main.remixWorld && ZoneDungeon)
		{
			SurfaceAtmospherics = false;
		}
		ZoneRain = Main.raining && SurfaceAtmospherics;

Tile 226 counts toward JungleTileCount, but the temple zone itself is just `wall == 87` at the center tile. Our SceneMetrics.ts already implements `zoneTemple: centerWall === 87` — that IS the vanilla formula, 1:1 already.

So for golem item 1: the task says "grep 原版 SceneMetrics.cs 对应 Zone 的 tile 计数公式" and "神庙砖 226 计数阈值查源码". The reality: there's no 226 count threshold — temple zone is `wall==87` at center. And jungle is `JungleTileCount >= 140 && !ZoneUnderworldHeight`. Our SceneMetrics has both. Good — so the plan is: SceneMetrics already has zoneTemple (wall 87, correct) and zoneJungle (count>=140, correct).

228	    }
229	    if (!losClear(game, vx80, vy80 - 30, player!.cx, player!.cy)) {
230	      // 隔墙:直接朝玩家 14 速 / 0.1 步进(:30890-30934)
231	      const dx = player!.cx - vx80, dy = player!.cy - vy80;
232	      const t = 14 / (Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1);
233	      e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, dx * t, 0.1);
234	      e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, dy * t, 0.1);
235	    } else if (num692 > 100) {
236	      // 朝悬浮点(玩家上方 300)逼近 4 速 / 0.05 步进(:30935-30978);
237	      // 原版此分支符号翻转时步进 ×2(num689 * 2f),与 accelTo 单补步不同 → 专用步进
238	      direction = player!.cx < e.cx ? -1 : 1;
239	      e.facing = direction;
240	      const t = num688 / num692;
241	      e.vx = step2(e.vx, num690 * t, num689);
242	      e.vy = step2(e.vy, num691 * t, num689);
243	    }
244	    const num703 = 20 - 5 * num658;
245	    if (e.ai1 > num693 * num703) {
246	      // 弹幕时长满(40*20=800t)→ 换招(:30979-30981);ai1=3 供 -1 去重
247	      e.ai0 = -1;
248	      e.ai1 = 3;
249	    }
250	  } else if (e.ai0 === 4) {
251	    // 远距追赶(:30982-30998):14 速混合逼近,进入 2000px → 换招
252	    la(e)[0] = 1;
253	    let tx = player!.cx - e.cx, ty = player!.cy - e.cy;
254	    const d = Math.hypot(tx, ty) || 1;
255	    tx = (tx / d) * 14;
256	    ty = (ty / d) * 14;
257	    e.vx = (e.vx * 14 + tx) / 15;
258	    e.vy = (e.vy * 14 + ty) / 15;
259	    direction = e.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1;
260	    e.facing = direction;
261	    if (num659 < 2000) {
262	      e.ai0 = -1;
263	      la(e)[0] = 0;
264	    }
265	  }
266	  e.x += e.vx;
267	  e.y += e.vy;
268	}

270	// ============================================================================
271	// AI_050 孢子漂移族(NPC.cs:31807-31872):NPC 261 真菌孢子 / 265 孢子(世纪之花二阶段产物)。
272	// 弱重力 0.02 下沉 + X 朝玩家 0.1 步进;261 撞墙即碎(life=0),265 穿墙。
273	// ============================================================================
274	export function planteraHookAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
275	  // EncourageDespawn(5):共享 Despawn 系统兜底
276	  e.vy += 0.02;                                    // :31819
277	  if (player) {
278	    if (e.vy < 0 && player.y > e.y + 100) e.vy *= 0.95;   // :31821-31824
279	    if (e.vy > 1) e.vy = 1;
280	    // X 朝玩家 0.1 步进,逆向先 0.98 阻尼(:31829-31860;expert 双档省略)
281	    if (e.x + e.w < player.x) {
282	      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
283	      e.vx += 0.1;
284	    } else if (e.x > player.x + player.w) {
285	      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
286	      e.vx -= 0.1;
287	    }
288	  }
289	  if (e.vx > 5 || e.vx < -5) e.vx *= 0.97;         // :31861-31864
290	  e.spin = e.vx * 0.2;                             // rotation = velocity.X * 0.2f(:31865)
291	  if (e.vanillaId === 261) {
292	    // 真菌孢子:noTileCollide=false,撞墙即碎(:31810-31818)
293	    e.onGround = false; e.hitWall = false; e.hitHead = false;
294	    moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
295	    if (e.hitWall || e.hitHead || e.onGround) {
296	      e.hp = 0;
297	      e.dead = true;
298	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#7A9E4F', 5, 1.5);   // HitEffect(0,100) 近似
299	    }
300	  } else {
301	    // 265 孢子 noTileCollide(:31815-31817)
302	    e.x += e.vx;
303	    e.y += e.vy;
304	  }
305	}

307	// ============================================================================
308	// AI_051 世纪之花 262(NPC.cs:31873-32237)
309	// 本体无重力,由钩蔓(263, aiStyle 52)均值牵引:目标点 = 钩蔓中心 + 朝玩家偏移(≤500px);
310	// 一阶段(血>50%):防 36 / 接触伤 50,种子弹幕 275(22)/276 毒种(27)/277 刺球(31);
311	// 二阶段(血≤50%):防 10 / 接触伤 70,提速(2.5→5→7 / 0.025→0.05),触须 264×8 + 孢子 265。
312	// plantBoss 静态引用由"场上 262 查找"等价替代(单 Boss 语义)。
313	// ============================================================================
314	export function planteraAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
315	  const l = la(e);
316	  // 玩家死(:31875-31883) → flag51 = 反向离场。框架在玩家死亡时传 null,
317	  // 而原版 flag51 分支仍读取尸体坐标 → 从 game 取回原对象(Enemy.eocAI 同款)
318	  const p = player ?? (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player ?? null;
319	  const flag51 = !p || p.dead;
320	  if (!p) {   // 原版不可能无 player(Main.player[target] 恒有对象);防御性减速漂移
321	    e.vx *= 0.95;
322	    e.vy *= 0.95;
323	    e.x += e.vx;
324	    e.y += e.vy;
325	    return;
326	  }
327	  // 暴怒 flag50(:31923-31928):玩家非丛林/高于地表/低于地狱层 → 省略(见汇报简化项)
328	  const flag50 = false;
329	  // 初始化(:31886-31892):localAI[0]=1 + 钩蔓 263×3
330	  if (!e.bInit) {
331	    e.bInit = true;
332	    l[0] = 1;
333	    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) spawnPart(game, e, 263);
334	  }
335	  // 钩蔓中心(:31893-31913):场上前 3 只 aiStyle 52 的位置均值
336	  let sumX = 0, sumY = 0, cnt = 0;
337	  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
338	    if (n !== e && !n.dead && n.vanilla?.aiStyle === 52) {
339	      sumX += n.cx;
340	      sumY += n.cy;
341	      cnt++;
342	      if (cnt > 2) break;   // 原版 num791 > 2 break(只取 3 只)
343	    }
344	  }
345	  // 原版 num791=0 时除 0 得 NaN(本体漂移);此处退化为本体位保持可玩
346	  let num789 = cnt > 0 ? sumX / cnt : e.cx;
347	  let num790 = cnt > 0 ? sumY / cnt : e.cy;
348	  // 速度/加速度档(:31914-31928):2.5/0.025 → 半血 5/0.05 → 1/4 血 7
349	  let num793 = 2.5;
350	  let num794 = 0.025;
351	  if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) { num793 = 5; num794 = 0.05; }
352	  if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num793 = 7;
353	  // flag50:num793 += 8 / num794 = 0.15;expert:+1 ×1.1 / +0.01 ×1.1 省略(:31930-31941)
354	  // 目标点 = 钩蔓中心 + 朝玩家偏移,钳 500px(:31942-31968)
355	  let num795 = p.cx - num789;
356	  let num796 = p.cy - num790;
357	  if (flag51) {
358	    num795 = -num795;
359	    num796 = -num796;
360	    num793 += 8;
361	  }
362	  let num797 = Math.hypot(num795, num796);
363	  const num798 = 500 + (flag50 ? 350 : 0);   // expert +150 省略
364	  if (num797 >= num798) {
365	    const s = num798 / num797;
366	    num795 *= s;
367	    num796 *= s;
368	  }
369	  num789 += num795;
370	  num790 += num796;
371	  // 逼近目标点(:31969-32015):距离 < num793 保持现速,否则归一到 num793;0.025 步进(符号翻转双步)
372	  num795 = num789 - e.cx;
373	  num796 = num790 - e.cy;
374	  num797 = Math.hypot(num795, num796);
375	  if (num797 < num793) {
376	    num795 = e.vx;
377	    num796 = e.vy;
378	  } else {
379	    const s = num793 / num797;
380	    num795 *= s;
381	    num796 *= s;
382	  }
383	  // 原版此段符号翻转时补步 ×2(num794 * 2f,:31984-32015)→ step2(同蜂后毒刺态)
384	  e.vx = step2(e.vx, num795, num794);
385	  e.vy = step2(e.vy, num796, num794);
386	  // 朝向玩家旋转(:32016-32019)
387	  e.spin = Math.atan2(p.cy - e.cy, p.cx - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
388	  e.x += e.vx;
389	  e.y += e.vy;
390	
391	  if (e.hp > e.maxHp / 2) {
392	    // ---- 一阶段(:32020-32133) ----
393	    e.def.defense = 36;                       // :32021
394	    e.def.damage = 50;                        // GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(50) 经典 = 50
395	    l[1] += 1;                                // :32034
396	    for (const th of [0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.6]) if (e.hp < e.maxHp * th) l[1] += 1;
397	    // flag50:+3 / expert:+1 / expert justHit 概率蓄力 / getGoodWorld:+1 均省略(:32051-32066)
398	    if (l[1] > 80) {
399	      l[1] = 0;
400	      // 视线检查(:32069-32078):Collision.CanHit(position,w,h,player,...) 近似
401	      const flag52 = losClear(game, e.x, e.y, p.x, p.y);
402	      if (flag52) {
403	        // 种子 275 / 毒种 276 / 刺球 277(:32080-32133)
404	        const num802 = 15;                    // expert 17 省略
405	        let num803 = p.cx - e.cx;
406	        let num804 = p.cy - e.cy;
407	        const t = num802 / (Math.hypot(num803, num804) || 1);
408	        num803 *= t;
409	        num804 *= t;
410	        let num806 = 22;
411	        let num807 = 275;
412	        let life = 300;
413	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.8 && rnd(4) === 0) {
414	          num806 = 27;                        // 毒种 276
415	          num807 = 276;
416	          l[1] = -30;                         // 下发弹延迟 30t
417	        } else if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.8 && rnd(8) === 0) {
418	          num806 = 31;                        // 刺球 277
419	          num807 = 277;
420	          l[1] = -120;                        // 下发弹延迟 120t
421	          life = 1200;                        // 原版不设 timeLeft(保持默认),此处取本框架默认
422	        }
423	        shoot(game, e.cx + num803 * 3, e.cy + num804 * 3, num803, num804, num806, num807, life);
424	      }
425	    }
426	  } else {
427	    // ---- 二阶段(血≤50%,:32134-32237) ----
428	    e.def.defense = 10;                       // :32134
429	    e.def.damage = 70;                        // GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(70) 经典 = 70
430	    // 首次进二阶段:触须 264×8(:32144-32170;expert 追加分配/getGoodWorld +6 省略)
431	    if (l[0] === 1) {
432	      l[0] = 2;
433	      for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) spawnPart(game, e, 264);
434	    }
435	    // 二阶段入场花瓣 Gore 378/379/380(:32194-32200)→ 粒子近似
436	    if (l[2] === 0) {
437	      l[2] = 1;
438	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#5FA054', 8, 2);
439	    }
440	    // expert 每 60t 补触须(:32172-32190)省略
441	    l[1] += 1;                                // :32202
442	    for (const th of [0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1]) if (e.hp < e.maxHp * th) l[1] += 1;
443	    if (l[1] >= 350) {
444	      // 孢子 265(:32216-32236):8 速,落点偏移(下方目标去掉 0.2|dx| 弧垂)
445	      const num820 = p.cx - e.cx + rndRange(-10, 11);
446	      let num821 = Math.abs(num820 * 0.2);
447	      let num822 = p.cy - e.cy + rndRange(-10, 11);
448	      if (num822 > 0) num821 = 0;
449	      num822 -= num821;
450	      const t = 8 / (Math.hypot(num820, num822) || 1);
451	      const spore = spawnPart(game, e, 265);
452	      if (spore) {
453	        spore.vx = num820 * t;
454	        spore.vy = num822 * t;
455	      }
456	      l[1] = 0;
457	    }
458	  }
459	}

461	// ============================================================================
462	// AI_052 世纪之花钩蔓 263(NPC.cs:32238-32405):抓墙锚定 + 牵引本体。
463	// ai[0]/ai[1] = 锚点 tile 坐标;localAI[0] = 换锚倒计时(每次抓墙后 300-600t,血低加速);
464	// 到点后钉在锚点(速度=残差),本体以钩蔓均值+玩家偏移为目标被拉动。
465	// plantBoss 静态引用由"场上 262 查找"等价替代;本体消失 → 自毁(StrikeNPCNoInteraction 9999)。
466	// ============================================================================
467	export function planteraTentacleAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
468	  const l = la(e);
469	  const st = game.world.store;
470	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
471	  // 本体引用(原版静态 plantBoss;:32241-32246 本体消失 → 9999 自伤)
472	  const boss = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 262) ?? null;
473	  if (!boss) {
474	    e.hp -= 9999;
475	    if (e.hp <= 0) e.dead = true;
476	    return;
477	  }
478	  e.master = boss; // 钩蔓链渲染用（Renderer.drawPlanteraVine，Main.cs:22225-22261）
479	  const target: { cx: number; cy: number; x: number; y: number; dead: boolean } = player ?? boss;
480	  const flag54 = !player || player.dead;    // 玩家死(:32248-32250)
481	  // 暴怒 flag53(玩家离丛林/地表/地狱层,localAI[0]-4/换锚-6/速度 ×2)省略(见汇报简化项)
482	  const flag53 = false;
483	  // 换锚倒计时(:32268-32291)
484	  if (e.ai0 === 0 || e.ai1 === 0) l[0] = 0;
485	  l[0] -= 1;
486	  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2) l[0] -= 2;   // :32275-32277
487	  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 4) l[0] -= 2;
488	  if (flag53) l[0] -= 4;                     // 原版入口 -4(:32252-32256)+ 换锚前 -6(:32283-32285)合并近似
489	  // 错峰:其他同类钩蔓仍在飞行 → 随机 60-300t 后再换(:32286-32298)
490	  if (!flag54 && l[0] <= 0 && e.ai0 !== 0) {
491	    for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
492	      if (n !== e && !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 263 && (n.vx !== 0 || n.vy !== 0)) {
493	        l[0] = rndRange(60, 300);
494	        break;
495	      }
496	    }
497	  }
498	  if (l[0] <= 0) {
499	    // 搜新锚点(:32299-32351):玩家附近(无锚时取玩家-本体中点)随机扩散找实心/墙 tile
500	    l[0] = rndRange(300, 600);
501	    let found = false;
502	    let num826 = 0;
503	    while (!found && num826 <= 1000) {
504	      num826++;
505	      let num827 = Math.floor(target.cx / TILE);
506	      let num828 = Math.floor(target.cy / TILE);
507	      if (e.ai0 === 0) {
508	        // 首锚:取玩家与本体中点(:32309-32312)
509	        num827 = Math.floor((target.cx + boss.cx) / 32);
510	        num828 = Math.floor((target.cy + boss.cy) / 32);
511	      }
512	      if (flag54) {
513	        num827 = Math.floor(boss.x / TILE);
514	        num828 = Math.floor((boss.y + 400) / TILE);
515	      }
516	      const num829 = 20 + Math.floor(100 * (num826 / 1000));   // 扩散半径 20→120
517	      let num830 = num827 + rndRange(-num829, num829 + 1);
518	      let num831 = num828 + rndRange(-num829, num829 + 1);
519	      // 半血后 1/6 概率直接试玩家所在格(有墙才用,:32322-32332)
520	      if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2 && rnd(6) === 0) {
521	        const ptx = Math.floor(target.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(target.cy / TILE);
522	        if (st.inBounds(ptx, pty) && st.wall[st.idx(ptx, pty)] > 0) {
523	          num830 = ptx;
524	          num831 = pty;
525	        }
526	      }
527	      // 实心 tile / (墙 tile 且搜索过半或本体半血)(:32333-32350)
528	      if (st.inBounds(num830, num831)
529	        && (st.isSolid(num830, num831)
530	          || (st.wall[st.idx(num830, num831)] > 0 && (num826 > 500 || boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2)))) {
531	        found = true;
532	        e.ai0 = num830;
533	        e.ai1 = num831;
534	      }
535	    }
536	  }
537	  if (!(e.ai0 > 0) || !(e.ai1 > 0)) return;   // :32352-32355 无锚不动
538	  // 收线速度(:32356-32375):6 → 本体半血 8 → 1/4 血 10(expert +1/+1、暴怒/玩家死 ×2)
539	  let num834 = 6;
540	  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2) num834 = 8;
541	  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 4) num834 = 10;
542	  if (flag54) num834 *= 2;
543	  // 朝锚点(锚点 tile 中心 - 8px)收线(:32383-32395):近于 12+速 → 速度=残差(钉住)
544	  const num835 = e.ai0 * TILE - 8 - e.cx;
545	  const num836 = e.ai1 * TILE - 8 - e.cy;
546	  const num837 = Math.hypot(num835, num836);
547	  if (num837 < 12 + num834) {
548	    e.vx = num835;
549	    e.vy = num836;
550	  } else {
551	    const s = num834 / num837;
552	    e.vx = num835 * s;
553	    e.vy = num836 * s;
554	  }
555	  // 朝本体旋转(:32396-32405)
556	  e.spin = Math.atan2(boss.cy - e.cy, boss.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;
557	  e.x += e.vx;
558	  e.y += e.vy;
559	}

561	// ============================================================================
562	// AI_053 世纪之花触须 264(NPC.cs:32406-32536):二阶段环绕本体漂移攻击。
563	// ai[0]/ai[1] = 环绕偏移(px,每 120-480t 随机重掷 ±100);环绕半径 200,
564	// 本体 <25%/10% 血各 +100;加速度 0.2(符号翻转 0.9 阻尼),速度钳 ±8;
565	// ai[3]>0 时锚定 ai[3]-1 指定的 NPC(原版多人分割);本体消失 → 自毁。
566	// ============================================================================
567	export function planteraTentacle2AI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
568	  const l = la(e);
569	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; l[0] = 0; }
570	  const boss = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 262) ?? null;
571	  if (!boss) { e.hp -= 9999; if (e.hp <= 0) e.dead = true; return; }
572	  // 原版 :32426-32429:ai[3]>0 → 锚 ai[3]-1 的 NPC(本框架触须不设锚,恒随本体)
573	  const anchor = e.ai3 > 0
574	    ? (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.id === e.ai3 - 1) ?? boss
575	    : boss;
576	  e.master = anchor; // 触须链渲染用（Renderer.drawPlanteraVine，Main.cs:22262-22285）
577	  // 偏移重掷(:32432-32439)
578	  l[0] -= 1;
579	  if (l[0] <= 0) {
580	    l[0] = rndRange(120, 480);
581	    e.ai0 = rndRange(-100, 101);
582	    e.ai1 = rndRange(-100, 101);
583	  }
584	  // 环绕半径随本体血量(:32442-32451;expert 动态半径省略)
585	  let orbit = 200;
586	  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp * 0.25) orbit += 100;
587	  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp * 0.1) orbit += 100;
588	  // 目标点 = 锚点 + 归一化偏移×半径;±0.2 步进,符号翻转 0.9 阻尼(:32467-32509)
589	  let dx = anchor.cx + e.ai0 - anchor.cx;
590	  let dy = anchor.cy + e.ai1 - anchor.cy;
591	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
592	  dx = (dx / d) * orbit;
593	  dy = (dy / d) * orbit;
594	  if (e.cx < anchor.cx + dx) { e.vx += 0.2; if (e.vx < 0 && dx > 0) e.vx *= 0.9; }
595	  else if (e.cx > anchor.cx + dx) { e.vx -= 0.2; if (e.vx > 0 && dx < 0) e.vx *= 0.9; }
596	  if (e.cy < anchor.cy + dy) { e.vy += 0.2; if (e.vy < 0 && dy > 0) e.vy *= 0.9; }
597	  else if (e.cy > anchor.cy + dy) { e.vy -= 0.2; if (e.vy > 0 && dy < 0) e.vy *= 0.9; }
598	  e.vx = Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, e.vx));
599	  e.vy = Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, e.vy));
600	  // 朝向旋转(:32526-32535)
601	  if (dx > 0) { e.facing = 1; e.spin = Math.atan2(dy, dx); }
602	  if (dx < 0) { e.facing = -1; e.spin = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + 3.14; }
603	  void player;
604	  e.x += e.vx;
605	  e.y += e.vy;
606	}

// Boss 召唤三件套映射测试：BGM 表（Main.cs:12162-12280）、pickMusic Boss 优先级、
// 音效例外（蜂后 Item_173——Sfx 表断言）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { MUSIC, BOSS_MUSIC, bossMusicFor, pickMusic, newMusicState } from '../src/data/Music';
import { EMPTY_SCENE } from '../src/world/SceneMetrics';

describe('Boss 专属 BGM（Main.cs:12162-12280 + 12474-12535）', () => {
  it('映射表逐项对照原版', () => {
    expect(bossMusicFor(4)).toBe(MUSIC.Boss1);            // EoC → 默认 Boss1
    expect(bossMusicFor(13)).toBe(99);                    // EoW
    expect(bossMusicFor(35)).toBe(104);                   // 骷髅王
    expect(bossMusicFor(50)).toBe(93);                    // 史莱姆王
    expect(bossMusicFor(113)).toBe(12);                   // 血肉墙 Boss2
    expect(bossMusicFor(125)).toBe(97);                   // 双子
    expect(bossMusicFor(127)).toBe(98);                   // Prime
    expect(bossMusicFor(134)).toBe(92);                   // 毁灭者
    expect(bossMusicFor(222)).toBe(96);                   // 蜂后
    expect(bossMusicFor(245)).toBe(17);                   // 石巨人
    expect(bossMusicFor(262)).toBe(24);                   // 世花
    expect(bossMusicFor(266)).toBe(13);                   // 克脑 Boss3
    expect(bossMusicFor(370)).toBe(58);                   // 猪鲨
    expect(bossMusicFor(398)).toBe(38);                   // 月总
    expect(bossMusicFor(439)).toBe(94);                   // 异教徒
    expect(bossMusicFor(636)).toBe(57);                   // 光皇
    expect(bossMusicFor(657)).toBe(56);                   // 史莱姆皇后
    expect(bossMusicFor(668)).toBe(90);                   // 鹿角怪
    expect(bossMusicFor(999)).toBe(MUSIC.Boss1);          // 未列出 boss → Boss1
    // 段 id 全覆盖（13/14/15 同曲）
    expect(BOSS_MUSIC[14]).toBe(BOSS_MUSIC[13]);
    expect(BOSS_MUSIC[15]).toBe(BOSS_MUSIC[13]);
  });

  it('pickMusic：Boss 曲先于一切群系分支（含地狱/城镇）', () => {
    const scene = { ...EMPTY_SCENE, townNPCCount: 0 } as never;
    const base = {
      scene, isDay: true,
      worldSurface: 300, rockLevel: 500, lavaLine: 1000,
      maxTilesX: 4200, centerX: 200 * 16, centerY: 1100 * 16, // 地狱深度
      state: newMusicState(),
    };
    const withBoss = pickMusic({ ...base, bossMusic: 96 } as never);
    expect(withBoss).toBe(96);                              // 蜂后曲压过地狱
    const without = pickMusic({ ...base, bossMusic: 0 } as never);
    expect(without).toBe(MUSIC.Underworld);                 // 无 boss 落地狱曲
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';

// AI 家族错误回退消除回归（aiStyle 0/17/19/23/25/39/41/44/56）
type DartLike = { vx: number; vy: number; damage: number };

function flatWorld(playerAt: { x: number; y: number }) {
  const w = new World(80, 80, 42, 'open');
  const st = w.store;
  for (let x = 10; x <= 70; x++) st.setTileSilent(x, 60, 2); // 地板 y=60
  const shots: DartLike[] = [];
  const hooks = {
    world: w,
    player: {
      x: playerAt.x * 16, y: playerAt.y * 16, w: 20, h: 42,
      cx: playerAt.x * 16, cy: playerAt.y * 16 + 21,
      dead: false, useTime: 0,
    },
    playSfx: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {}, spawnParticles: () => {},
    spawnDrop: () => null, damagePlayer: () => {},
    entities: { add: (e: unknown) => { shots.push(e as DartLike); } },
  } as never;
  return { w, hooks, shots };
}

describe('AI 家族回退消除', () => {
  it('被缚NPC 105（aiStyle 0）：原地站立不动、零接触伤害', () => {
    const { hooks } = flatWorld({ x: 40, y: 58 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(105, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    expect(e.def.damage).toBe(0);          // friendly 旗 → 零伤害
    const x0 = Math.round(e.x);
    for (let t = 0; t < 120; t++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
    expect(Math.abs(e.x - x0)).toBeLessThan(1); // 不游走
  });

  it('秃鹫 61（aiStyle 17）：栖息不动；玩家进入 200px 盒 → 起飞（vy=-6）', () => {
    const far = flatWorld({ x: 60, y: 40 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(61, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    e.y = 60 * 16 - e.h; e.vy = 0;   // 贴地栖息（悬空出生原版语义=直接起飞）
    for (let t = 0; t < 60; t++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, far.hooks);
    expect(e.ai0).toBe(0);                  // 远处保持栖息
    const near = flatWorld({ x: 40, y: 58 });
    const e2 = Enemy.fromVanilla(61, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    let tookOff = false;
    for (let t = 0; t < 30; t++) {
      e2.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, near.hooks);
      if (e2.ai0 === 1) { tookOff = true; break; }
    }
    expect(tookOff).toBe(true);
  });

  it('蚁狮 69（aiStyle 19）：扎地不动；玩家在上方 200t 后射沙球（速 12、伤 10）', () => {
    const { hooks, shots } = flatWorld({ x: 40, y: 55 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(69, 40 * 16, 58 * 16)!;
    const x0 = Math.round(e.x);
    let fired = false;
    for (let t = 0; t < 400 && !fired; t++) {
      e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
      if (shots.length > 0) fired = true;
    }
    expect(fired).toBe(true);
    expect(Math.abs(e.x - x0)).toBeLessThan(2); // 全程扎根不位移
    expect(shots[0].damage).toBe(10);
    expect(Math.hypot(shots[0].vx, shots[0].vy)).toBeCloseTo(12, 1);
  });

  it('飞行武器 83（aiStyle 23）：三态循环——冲刺(9)→漂移→蓄转(转速递增)', () => {
    const { hooks } = flatWorld({ x: 44, y: 58 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(83, 40 * 16, 58 * 16)!;
